Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The stochastic operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 616. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 787 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the stochastic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^616 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 787-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
